Projection function: Use exact sum. Instead of iterative clip scaling, use a faster water-filling:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(50):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h
          tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          # add diff proportionally to rooms, but clipping; iterate
          h=np.clip(h+(diff/tot)*room,0,1)
       else: ...
    # final fine adjust: find free points, distribute
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if h.sum()!=target:
       free=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
       if free.any():
          val=np.clip(h[free]+diff/free.sum(),0,1)
          h[free]=val
          # if still off, adjust a single point
          diff=target-h.sum(); idx=np.argmin(np.abs(h - diff?))
       else: # all saturated, adjust one
          if diff>0: idx=np.argmin(h); h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff)
          else: idx=np.argmax(h); h[idx]=max(0,h[idx]+diff)
    return h
```

Alternatively implement our own projected gradient using `eval_smooth` gradient. Simpler, no SciPy overhead, can do many iterations with backtracking C. Let's design a custom Adam/gradient descent:
```
def grad_search(h,n,K,alpha,nu,steps,deadline):
   x=project(h)
   best_c=C
   for it:
       f,g=eval_smooth(x,n,K,alpha,nu)
       # project gradient onto sum? Since penalty handles sum; but to maintain, project after step
       # adaptive step using Armijo on actual C maybe
       step = 0.5 / max(1,np.linalg.norm(g,inf))
       for tries:
          y=project(x - step*g,n)
          cy=C(y)
          if cy < best_c or cy < current_c - 1e-12: break
          step*=0.5
       if improved: update
       if stagnation: break
```
But gradient of smooth top penalty may not decrease C quickly. We can add noise. Coordinate descent better.

`smooth_fun_and_grad`:
- x=clip(x,0,1)
- c=correlate(x,1-x)
- m=c.max()
- if K>=c.size: top = np.arange(c.size)
  else: top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]? Need keep top K; c.size ~1400, argsort fine.
- scaled = alpha*(c[top]-m); if alpha large overflow; use max scaled 0. w=np.exp(scaled); wsum=w.sum(); if wsum==0 weights uniform? scaled max0 so at least some.
- f = m + np.log(wsum)/alpha + nu*(x.sum()-n/2)**2
- grad = np.zeros(n)
- For each idx, weight = w_i/wsum, update as above.
- Add penalty grad.
Return f,grad. Note f in raw units? C=dx*raw. We can multiply objective by dx to align. derivative also dx. penalties scaled raw? Use raw and penalty weight. Since C lower, minimize raw equivalent. Penalty nu tune.

Gradient update loop top K maybe 80. Need `d = idx - (n-1)` and valid slices. Use local h vector. For each top, if d==0: grad += weight*(1-2*x)
elif d>0:
  l = n-d
  grad[:l] += weight*(1-x[d:])
  grad[d:] -= weight*x[:l]
elif d<0:
  p=-d; l=n-p
  grad[p:] += weight*(1-x[:l])
  grad[:l] -= weight*x[p:]
This O(K*n). Need avoid adding same for multiple top; loop.

But top K from c raw; c raw sum values ~/dx? Actually c entries up to n/2; f raw. C=dx*f. We can multiply by dx in objective; gradient by dx. We want enforce sum exact; use raw sum penalty. Bound step? L-BFGS uses objective magnitude. Use `f = scale*smooth_raw + pen_weight*(sum-target)**2`, grad= scale*g + 2*pen_weight*(sum-target). Good.

Pseudo:
```
def cd_fast(h,n,max_passes,deadline,seed=1,inner_iters=22):
    h=np.array(h,float); c=raw_corr(h); best=h.copy(); best_c=compute_raw_max(c)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for pass in range(max_passes):
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        h_sum_pass = h.sum()
        improved=False
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        cur_max = float(c.max())
        for p in order:
            x0=h[p]
            m_min = n-1-p
            m_max = 2*n-2-p
            if m_max < 0 or m_min >= 2*n-1: continue
            m = np.arange(m_min, m_max+1)  # allocate
            d = m - (n-1)
            A = 1.0 - h[p + d]
            b = p - d
            vb = (b >= 0) & (b < n)
            if np.any(vb): A[vb] -= h[b[vb]]
            diag_mask = (d == 0)
            has_diag=bool(np.any(diag_mask))
            if has_diag: A[diag_mask]=0.0
            # base for non diag
            idx_nd = ~diag_mask
            if np.any(idx_nd):
                base_nd = c[ m[idx_nd] ] - x0 * A[idx_nd]
                a_nd = A[idx_nd]
            else: base_nd empty
            unaff = -np.inf
            if m_min >0: unaff=max(unaff, c[:m_min].max())
            if m_max+1 < 2*n-1: unaff=max(unaff, c[m_max+1:].max())
            # lower bound
            lo_bound = unaff
            if has_diag:
                # min of base_diag+x-x^2 over [0,1] at x=0 or1: base or base
                # base_diag = cdiag - x0 + x0^2
                base_diag = c[n-1] - x0 + x0*x0  # m==n-1 in affected range? For p, diag index n-1 always within [m_min,m_max]? m_min=n-1-p <=n-1<=n-1+p? max? yes max=2n-2-p, for p? if p small n-1-p < n-1; if p large m_min >? p=n-1 m_min=0 <=n-1; so yes.
                lo_bound=max(lo_bound, base_diag) # min ends base_diag
            if np.any(idx_nd):
                # line min over [0,1] base + a x
                line_min = np.where(a_nd > 0, base_nd, np.minimum? Wait if a>0 min at x=0 base; a<0 min at x=1 base+a; a=0 base.
                lm = np.where(a_nd > 0, base_nd, np.where(a_nd < 0, base_nd + a_nd, base_nd))
                lo_bound=max(lo_bound, float(lm.max()))
            hi_bound = cur_max  # current x feasible
            if hi_bound <= lo_bound + 1e-15:
                x_new=x0
            else:
                Llo, Lhi = lo_bound, hi_bound
                for _ in range(inner_iters):
                    L=(Llo+Lhi)/2
                    ok=True
                    if unaff > L + 1e-13: ok=False
                    if ok and np.any(idx_nd):
                        lo=0.0; hi=1.0
                        pos = a_nd > 1e-14
                        neg = a_nd < -1e-14
                        zero = ~(pos|neg)
                        if np.any(zero) and np.any(base_nd[zero] > L + 1e-13): ok=False
                        if ok and np.any(pos):
                            hi = min(hi, float(np.min((L - base_nd[pos]) / a_nd[pos])))
                        if ok and np.any(neg):
                            lo = max(lo, float(np.max((L - base_nd[neg]) / a_nd[neg])))
                        if lo > hi + 1e-13: ok=False
                    if ok and has_diag:
                        disc = 1.0 - 4.0*(L - base_diag)
                        if disc >= 0:
                            r1=(1-np.sqrt(disc))*0.5; r2=(1+np.sqrt(disc))*0.5
                            # need line interval [lo,hi] defined; if no lines lo=0 hi=1
                            if lo > r1 and hi < r2:
                                ok=False
                L = Lhi
                # find x
                lo=0.0; hi=1.0
                if np.any(idx_nd): compute lo hi from L (same)
                # feasible candidates
                cands=[]
                if not has_diag or (1.0 - 4.0*(L-base_diag) < 0): # all diag feasible
                    xv=np.clip(x0,lo,hi); cands.append(xv)
                else:
                    disc=...
                    r1,r2
                    if lo <= r1:
                        x1=min(hi,r1); if x1>=lo-1e-12: cands.append(np.clip(x1,lo,hi))
                    if hi >= r2:
                        x2=max(lo,r2); if x2<=hi+1e-12: cands.append(np.clip(x2,lo,hi))
                    # if current feasible? not necessary
                if not cands:
                    x_new = np.clip(x0,0,1)
                else:
                    x_new = min(cands, key=lambda y: abs(y-x0)) # no lambda? use manual
                # Nonlambda: if len one else compare
            if abs(x_new - x0) > 1e-12:
                x_new=float(np.clip(x_new,0,1))
                # update c affected
                vals = c[m].copy()? We can compute all vals using base total.
                # For non diag: new = base_nd + a_nd*x_new
                # Need map back.
                # Easier: create vals_aff = np.empty(n)
                vals_aff = c[m] + (x_new - x0)*A  # this works for non diag; for diag A=0, doesn't update diagonal.
                if has_diag:
                    vals_aff[diag_mask] = base_diag + x_new - x_new*x_new
                # Also for non diag, c + (x-x0)*A correct? Let's verify: Non diag old contribution coefficient A includes (1-h_f) - h_b. New-old = (x-x0)A. Yes.
                c[m] = vals_aff
                h[p]=x_new
                aff_max = float(vals_aff.max())
                cur_max = max(unaff, aff_max)
                improved=True
        if improved:
            # special: if sum drift large? project
            h = project_feasible(h,n)
            c = raw_corr(h)
            cur_c = float(c.max())
            if cur_c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c=cur_c; best=h.copy()
        else:
            # maybe break after a few no-improve passes? We used order random; break if not improved.
            break
    # final project and evaluate
    h=project_feasible(h,n); c=raw_corr(h); cur_c=float(c.max())
    if cur_c<best_c: best...
    return best, compute_c5(best,n)
```

Random binary projection preserving sum:
```
def binary_threshold(h,n):
   k=n//2
   idx=np.argpartition(-h,k-1)[:k] if k>0 else []
   out=np.zeros(n); out[idx]=1; return out
```
This uses top positions; if n odd? use n//2.

```
def random_run_binary(n, rng, p_stop=0.3):
    h=np.zeros(n); i=0; val=1 if rng.rand()<0.5 else 0
    while i<n:
       L=int(rng.geometric(4,p=...)) maybe use exponential: L=max(1,int(rng.exponential(4)));
       h[i:min(n,i+L)]=val; i+=L; val=1-val
    return binary_threshold(h,n)
```

```
       h2,c2=cd_fast(h_int,n_try, max_passes=200 if n_try<=200 else 80 if <=400 else 40, deadline=deadline_sub, seed=seed+n_try)

    return best_h, compute_c5(best_h,n), n
```

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c5(h):
    n=len(h); return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'))*2/n)

def project_feasible(h,n): ...

def smooth_core(x,n,K,alpha,nu): ...

def smooth_objective(x,n,K,alpha,nu): return smooth_core(...)[0]
def smooth_gradient(x,n,K,alpha,nu): return smooth_core(...)[1]

def lbfgs_polish(h,n,deadline, configs=None): ...

def interp_to(h,n_new): ...
def binary_threshold(h,n): ...
def random_start(n,kind,rand): ...

def cd_fast(h,n,max_passes,deadline,seed=0,iters=16, project_every=1):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full').astype(float)
    omh=1-h
    best=h.copy(); best_c=float(c.max())
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    pass_no=0
    for _ in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>=deadline: break
       improved=False
       order=rng.permutation(n)
       cur_max=best_c? Actually maintain cur_max=float(c.max())
       for p in order:
           x0=h[p]
           m_min=n-1-p
           m_max=m_min+n-1
           aff=c[m_min:m_max+1]
           A=omh.copy()
           b_start=2*p - (n-1)
           if b_start<0: b_start=0
           b_end=2*p
           if b_end>=n: b_end=n-1
           if b_start<=b_end:
               pos_first=2*p - b_end
               pos_last=2*p - b_start
               # ensure slices within 0..n-1; pos_last≤? For p near n-1, pos_last =2p-(2p-n+1)=n-1. pos_first maybe? yes.
               A[pos_first:pos_last+1] -= h[b_end:b_start-1:-1]
           A[p]=0.0
           base_diag=aff[p] - x0 + x0*x0
           # line arrays
           # create a_non base using np.concatenate avoids modifying aff
           if p==0:
               a_non=A[1:]; base_non=aff[1:] - x0*A[1:]
           elif p==n-1:
               a_non=A[:-1]; base_non=aff[:-1] - x0*A[:-1]
           else:
               a_non=np.concatenate((A[:p], A[p+1:]))
               base_non=np.concatenate((aff[:p] - x0*A[:p], aff[p+1:] - x0*A[p+1:]))
           unaff=-np.inf
           if m_min>0: unaff=max(unaff, float(c[:m_min].max()))
           if m_max+1<2*n-1: unaff=max(unaff, float(c[m_max+1:].max()))
           lo_bound=unaff
           # diag min base_diag
           if base_diag>lo_bound: lo_bound=base_diag
           if a_non.size:
               # min line
               # vectorized min: where a>0 base, a<0 base+a, else base
               lm = base_non.copy()
               pos_a = a_non > 0.0
               lm[pos_a] = base_non[pos_a]  # already
               neg_a = a_non < 0.0
               lm[neg_a] = base_non[neg_a] + a_non[neg_a]
               zmax = float(lm.max()) if lm.size else -1e300
               if zmax>lo_bound: lo_bound=zmax
           cur_max_global = float(c.max())? We want Lhi current x feasible. Instead use current maximum = max(unaff, aff.max()) where aff current = current values. aff.max() known. Use `cur_local_max = max(unaff, float(aff.max()))` This should equal global cur if maintained; but if c global max outside? unaff included. Use hi_bound=cur_local_max.
           hi_bound=cur_local_max
           if hi_bound - lo_bound <= 1e-13:
               x_new=x0
           else:
               Llo, Lhi = lo_bound, hi_bound
               # precompute masks for a_non to speed
               eps=1e-14
               pos = a_non > eps
               neg = a_non < -eps
               zero = ~(pos|neg)
               base_pos = a_non[pos]? Wait name conflict; base_pos_arr = base_non[pos]; a_pos_arr=a_non[pos]
               ...
               for it in range(iters):
                   L=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
                   ok=True
                   if unaff > L+1e-13: ok=False
```

```
def random_start(n, kind, rand):
    x = np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
    if kind==0: return np.full(n,0.5)
    if kind==1: out=zeros; idx=rand.choice(n, replace=False,size=n//2); out[idx]=1; return out
    if kind==2: return rand.rand(n)
    if kind==3: freq=1+rand.randint(0,5); return 0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x+rand.rand()*2*np.pi)
    if kind==4: random run binary:
       out=zeros; val=rand.rand()<0.5; i=0
       while i<n: L=max(1,int(rand.exponential(3))+1); out[i:min(n,i+L)]=val; i+=L; val=1-val
       return out
    if kind==5: try a low-high blocks: maybe out = 1 if (x % total) < block? Use random phases.
```

Let's draft final code in mind:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c5(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float); n=len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0/n))

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(80):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-14: break
          h=np.clip(h+(diff/tot)*room,0,1)
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-14: break
          h=np.clip(h+(diff/tot)*h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       free=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
       if free.any():
          h[free]=np.clip(h[free]+diff/free.sum(),0,1)
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)>1e-12:
          if diff>0:
             idx=int(np.argmin(h)); h[idx]=min(1.0,h[idx]+diff)
          else:
             idx=int(np.argmax(h)); h[idx]=max(0.0,h[idx]+diff)
    return h
```
Maybe final adjust with argmin can overshoot if diff>1? But flip saturations; loop should handle. Good.

`interp_to`:
```
def interp_to(h,n_new):
    n=len(h); if n==n_new: return h.copy()
    x=np.arange(n)*(2.0/n); y=np.arange(n_new)*(2.0/n_new)
    return np.interp(y,x,h)
```

Let's write cd_fast most carefully.
```
def cd_fast(h,n,max_passes,deadline,seed=0,iters=16):
    h=project_feasible(np.asarray(h,float),n)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full').astype(float)
    omh=1.0-h
    best=h.copy(); best_c=float(c.max())
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() >= deadline: break
        improved=False
        cur_max=float(c.max())
        for p in rng.permutation(n):
            x0=h[p]
            m_min=n-1-p
            m_max=m_min+n-1
            aff=c[m_min:m_max+1]
            A=omh.copy()
            bs=2*p - (n-1)
            if bs<0: bs=0
            be=2*p
            if be>=n: be=n-1
            if bs<=be:
                pf=2*p - be
                pl=2*p - bs
                A[pf:pl+1] -= h[be:bs-1:-1]
            A[p]=0.0
            base_diag=aff[p] - x0 + x0*x0
            a_non=np.empty(n-1,float); base_non=np.empty(n-1,float)
            if p:
                a_non[:p]=A[:p]; base_non[:p]=aff[:p] - x0*A[:p]
            if p<n-1:
                a_non[p:]=A[p+1:]; base_non[p:]=aff[p+1:] - x0*A[p+1:]
            unaff=-1e300
            if m_min>0:
                um=float(c[:m_min].max()); unaff=um if um>unaff else unaff
            if m_max+1<2*n-1:
                um=float(c[m_max+1:].max()); unaff=um if um>unaff else unaff
            lo_bound=unaff
            if base_diag>lo_bound: lo_bound=base_diag
            if n>1:
                lm=base_non.copy()
                neg=base_non? Use arrays.
                negmask=a_non<0
                if np.any(negmask): lm[negmask] = base_non[negmask] + a_non[negmask]
                zero? pos min base already. Need for a=0 base already. For a>0 base. For a<0 base+a. So lm=base_non; for neg set base+a. But for a>0 min base; correct. For a=0 base. So:
                lm = base_non.copy(); negm=a_non<0; lm[negm]=base_non[negm]+a_non[negm]
                zmax=float(lm.max()); if zmax>lo_bound: lo_bound=zmax
            hi_bound=max(unaff, float(aff.max()))
            if hi_bound <= lo_bound + 1e-12:
                x_new=x0
            else:
                eps=1e-14
                pos=a_non>eps; neg=a_non<-eps; zero=~(pos|neg)
                bp=base_non[pos]; ap=a_non[pos]; bn=base_non[neg]; an=a_non[neg]; bz=base_non[zero]
                Llo=Lhi=... 
                for _ in range(iters):
                   L=...
                   ok=True
                   if unaff > L+1e-12: ok=False
                   lo=0.0; hi=1.0
                   if ok and zero.any():
                       if float(bz.max()) > L+1e-12: ok=False
                   if ok and pos.any():
                       v=float(np.min((L-bp)/ap)); hi = v if v<hi else hi
                   if ok and neg.any():
                       v=float(np.max((L-bn)/an)); lo = v if v>lo else lo
                   if ok and lo>hi+1e-12: ok=False
                   if ok:
                       disc=1-4*(L-base_diag)
                       if disc>=0:
                           rt=np.sqrt(disc); r1=0.5*(1-rt); r2=0.5*(1+rt)
                           if lo>r1 and hi<r2: ok=False
                   if ok: Lhi=L
                   else: Llo=L
                L=Lhi
                # final line bounds
                lo=0.0; hi=1.0
                if pos.any(): hi=min(hi, float(np.min((L-bp)/ap)))
                if neg.any(): lo=max(lo, float(np.max((L-bn)/an)))
                # zero no bound
                cands=[]
                disc=1-4*(L-base_diag)
                if disc < 0:
                    cands.append(float(np.clip(x0, lo, hi)))
                else:
                    rt=np.sqrt(disc); r1=...; r2=...
                    if lo <= r1 + 1e-12:
                        xv=min(hi, r1)
                        if xv >= lo - 1e-12:
                            cands.append(float(np.clip(xv, 0.0, 1.0)))
                    if hi >= r2 - 1e-12:
                        xv=max(lo, r2)
                        if xv <= hi + 1e-12:
                            cands.append(float(np.clip(xv, 0.0, 1.0)))
                    if not cands:
                        cands.append(float(np.clip(x0, lo, hi)))
                # choose closest
                best_x=x0; bestd=abs(x0-x0)
                for cv in cands:
                    d=abs(cv-x0)
                    if d<bestd: bestd=d; best_x=cv
                x_new=float(np.clip(best_x,0,1))
            if abs(x_new-x0)>1e-12:
                new_aff=aff + (x_new-x0)*A
                new_aff[p]=base_diag + x_new - x_new*x_new
                c[m_min:m_max+1]=new_aff
                h[p]=x_new; omh[p]=1.0-x_new
                improved=True
        if improved:
            h=project_feasible(h,n)
            c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full').astype(float)
            omh=1-h
            cc=float(c.max())
            if cc<best_c:
                best_c=cc; best=h.copy()
        else:
            break
    h=project_feasible(h,n); c=...; cc=...
    if cc<best_c: ...
    return best, compute_c5(best,n)
```

Now, L-BFGS smooth core. Need multiply by scale=2/n. Use c raw and smooth logsum over top K. For gradient, weights normalized. If K=c.size, top order all. Sorting each eval; for n=700 c size1399, top 80, okay. Potential issue with `np.argpartition` faster but argsort fine.
```
def smooth_core(x,n,K,alpha,nu):
    x=np.clip(np.asarray(x,float),0,1)
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full')
    m=float(c.max())
    if K<c.size:
       order=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:] if K>0 else np.empty(0)
    else: order=np.arange(c.size)
    shift=alpha*(c[order]-m)
    ex=np.exp(shift)
    w=ex/ex.sum()
    f=m + np.log(ex.sum())/alpha
    scale=2.0/n
    sdiff=x.sum()-0.5*n
    f=scale*f + nu*sdiff*sdiff
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for idx,wt in zip(order,w):
       d=idx-(n-1)
       if d==0: g += wt*(1-2*x)
       elif d>0:
           l=n-d
           g[:l] += wt*(1-x[d:])
           g[d:] -= wt*x[:l]
       else:
           p=-d; l=n-p
           g[p:] += wt*(1-x[:l])
           g[:l] -= wt*x[p:]
    g = scale*g + 2*nu*sdiff
    return f,g
```
Check gradient for d<0: d=-p, c = sum_{i=p}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-p}). Derivative first (i=j) adds (1-h_{j-p}) for j=p..n-1. second (i=j+p) subtracts h_{j+p} for j=0..n-p-1. Code: g[p:] += wt*(1-x[:l]) where l=n-p. For j=p+i, g[j]+=1-x[i]. Good. g[:l] -= wt*x[p:] (i=0..l-1, g[i]-=x[p+i]). Good.
For d>0: g[:l]+=wt*(1-x[d:]) ; g[d:]-=wt*x[:l]. Good.
For d=0: c=sum x(1-x), grad=1-2x. Code okay.
We add position d=0 top maybe multiple? only one index n-1.

L-BFGS polish:
```
def lbfgs_polish(h,n,deadline,configs=None):
    best=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(best)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    if configs is None: configs=[(60,2000,10.0*n),(100,8000,30.0*n)]
    for K,alpha,nu in configs:
       if time.time()>deadline-2: break
       try:
          res=minimize(smooth_objective,best,args=(n,K,alpha,nu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,jac=smooth_gradient,options={'maxiter':60,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-7,'maxfun':100})
          hp=project_feasible(res.x,n); cp=compute_c5(hp)
          if cp<best_c: best=hp; best_c=cp
       except Exception: pass
    return best,best_c
```
minimize with `jac=smooth_gradient` requires same args. Good.

`random_start` implementation top-level. It uses rng; no closures.
```
def random_start(n, kind, rng):
    if n<=0: return np.array([])
    x=np.arange(n)*(2.0/n)
    if kind==0: return np.full(n,0.5)
    if kind==1:
       out=np.zeros(n); k=n//2; idx=rng.choice(n,k,replace=False); out[idx]=1.0; return out
    if kind==2: return rng.uniform(0,1,n)
    if kind==3:
       freq=1.0+rng.randint(0,5)*0.5; ph=rng.uniform(0,6.28); return 0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x+ph)
    if kind==4:
       out=np.zeros(n); val=1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else 0.0; i=0
       while i<n:
          L=max(1,int(rng.exponential(3.0))+1)
          out[i:min(n,i+L)]=val; i+=L; val=1.0-val
       return out
    if kind==5:
       # blocks of ones and zeros with random block lengths and start
       out=zeros; i=0; val=rng.random()<0.5
       while i<n: L=rng.randint(3,15); ...
       return out
    return np.full(n,0.5)
```
Use rng.exponential; RandomState has exponential. Good. But if kind4 sum not n/2, project.

Run code. Need maybe use `evaluate_erdos_solution`? Not necessary.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+float(budget_s)-5
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        n0=len(initial_h_values); h0=np.asarray(initial_h_values,float).copy()
    except Exception:
        n0=200; h0=np.full(200,0.5)
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
    best=h0.copy(); n_best=n0; best_c=compute_c5(best)
    # initial CD
    if time.time()<deadline-100:
       h2,c2=cd_fast(best,n_best,max_passes=40,deadline=min(deadline-80,time.time()+180),seed=seed)
       if c2<best_c: best,n_best,best_c=h2,n2,c2
    # small/multiscale exploration
    ns=[64,100,160,240,360,560,800]
    # include n_best maybe
    for n_try in ns:
       if time.time()>deadline-120: break
       if n_try==n_best: continue
       # choose number of bases
       max_passes=1000 if n_try<=100 else 400 if n_try<=200 else 150 if n_try<=400 else 60
       time_per=25 if n_try<=128 else 40 if n_try<=320 else 60
       bases=[interp_to(best,n_best,n_try)]
       kinds=range(5) if n_try<=160 else range(3)
       for k in kinds:
           if time.time()>deadline-150: break
           if n_try>300 and k>0: break? Maybe.
           base=project_feasible(bases[k] if k==0 else random_start(n_try,k+1? ),n_try)
           dl=min(time.time()+time_per, deadline-120)
           h2,c2=cd_fast(base,n_try,max_passes,dl,seed=seed+n_try+k)
           if c2<best_c:
               best,n_best,best_c=h2,n_try,c2
               # maybe immediately upsample to original? continue
    # after exploration, polish current best at its n with more CD
    if time.time()<deadline-80:
       maxp = 300 if n_best<=150 else 150 if n_best<=300 else 60 if n_best<=600 else 30
       h2,c2=cd_fast(best,n_best,maxp,deadline-60,seed=seed+1234)
       if c2<best_c: update
    # L-BFGS polish
    if time.time()<deadline-60:
       h2,c2=lbfgs_polish(best,n_best,deadline-40,[ (50,2000,10*n_best), (80,8000,30*n_best)])
       if c2<best_c: update
    # random restarts perturbations
    if time.time()<deadline-40:
       for r in range(40):
          if time.time()>deadline-30: break
          s=0.008+0.003*r
          hp=project_feasible(best+rng.normal(0,s,n_best),n_best)
          # alternate with binary threshold for some
          if r%5==4: hp=project_feasible(binary_threshold(hp,n_best),n_best)
          maxp=100 if n_best<=200 else 30 if n_best<=500 else 15
          dl=min(time.time()+ (30 if n_best<=200 else 15 if n_best<=500 else 10), deadline-30)
          h2,c2=cd_fast(hp,n_best,maxp,dl,seed=5000+r)
          if c2<best_c: update
          # if found much better, maybe LBFGS
    # final CD
    if time.time()<deadline-3:
       maxp=200 if n_best<=100 else 50 if n_best<=300 else 20
       h2,c2=cd_fast(best,n_best,maxp,deadline-1,seed=999)
       if c2<best_c: update
    h_best=project_feasible(best,n_best)
    c_best=compute_c5(h_best)
    return h_best, c_best, n_best
```

```
def binary_threshold(h,n):
   out=np.zeros(n); k=n//2
   if k>0:
      idx=np.argpartition(h, n-1-k)[-k:]  # top k
      out[idx]=1
   return out
```

Detailed final adjust:
```
diff=target-h.sum()
if abs(diff)>1e-10:
    free=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
    if free.any(): h[free]=np.clip(h[free]+diff/free.sum(),0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-10:
       # repeat maybe 5 times
       for _ in range(5):
          if diff>0:
             idx=np.argmin(h); add=min(1-h[idx],diff); h[idx]+=add; diff-=add
          else:
             idx=np.argmax(h); sub=min(h[idx],-diff); h[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
          if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
```
This ensures exact. Add few lines.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 1.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / n))

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    for _ in range(8):
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        free = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if free.any():
            h[free] = np.clip(h[free] + diff / free.sum(), 0.0, 1.0)
            diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            i = int(np.argmin(h))
            add = min(1.0 - h[i], diff)
            h[i] += add
            diff -= add
        else:
            i = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(h[i], -diff)
            h[i] -= sub
            diff += sub
    return h

def interp_to(h, n_new):
    n = len(h)
    if n == n_new:
        return np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    x = np.arange(n) * (2.0 / n)
    y = np.arange(n_new) * (2.0 / n_new)
    return np.interp(y, x, np.asarray(h, dtype=float))

def binary_threshold(h, n):
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    k = n // 2
    if k > 0:
        idx = np.argpartition(h, n - 1 - k)[-k:]
        out[idx] = 1.0
    return out

def random_start(n, kind, rng):
    x = np.arange(n) * (2.0 / n)
    if kind == 0:
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    if kind == 1:
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=n // 2, replace=False)
        out[idx] = 1.0
        return out
    if kind == 2:
        return rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
    if kind == 3:
        freq = 1.0 + 0.5 * rng.randint(0, 6)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 6.283185307179586)
        return 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    val = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else 0.0
    i = 0
    while i < n:
        L = max(1, int(rng.exponential(3.0)) + 1)
        out[i:min(n, i + L)] = val
        i += L
        val = 1.0 - val
    return out

def cd_fast(h, n, max_passes, deadline, seed=0, iters=16):
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full").astype(float)
    omh = 1.0 - h
    best = h.copy()
    best_raw = float(c.max())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    tol = 1e-12
    for _ in range(int(max_passes)):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        improved = False
        for p in rng.permutation(n):
            x0 = h[p]
            m_min = n - 1 - p
            m_max = m_min + n - 1
            aff = c[m_min:m_max + 1]
            A = omh.copy()
            bs = 2 * p - (n - 1)
            if bs < 0:
                bs = 0
            be = 2 * p
            if be >= n:
                be = n - 1
            if bs <= be:
                pf = 2 * p - be
                pl = 2 * p - bs
                A[pf:pl + 1] -= h[be:bs - 1:-1]
            A[p] = 0.0
            base_diag = aff[p] - x0 + x0 * x0
            a_non = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=float)
            b_non = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=float)
            if p:
                a_non[:p] = A[:p]
                b_non[:p] = aff[:p] - x0 * A[:p]
            if p < n - 1:
                a_non[p:] = A[p + 1:]
                b_non[p:] = aff[p + 1:] - x0 * A[p + 1:]
            unaff = -1e300
            if m_min > 0:
                um = float(c[:m_min].max())
                if um > unaff:
                    unaff = um
            if m_max + 1 < 2 * n - 1:
                um = float(c[m_max + 1:].max())
                if um > unaff:
                    unaff = um
            lo_bound = unaff
            if base_diag > lo_bound:
                lo_bound = base_diag
            if n > 1:
                lm = b_non.copy()
                neg_a = a_non < 0.0
                if np.any(neg_a):
                    lm[neg_a] = b_non[neg_a] + a_non[neg_a]
                zmax = float(lm.max())
                if zmax > lo_bound:
                    lo_bound = zmax
            hi_bound = max(unaff, float(aff.max()))
            if hi_bound <= lo_bound + 1e-13:
                x_new = x0
            else:
                eps = 1e-14
                pos = a_non > eps
                neg = a_non < -eps
                zero = ~(pos | neg)
                bp = b_non[pos]
                ap = a_non[pos]
                bn = b_non[neg]
                an = a_non[neg]
                bz = b_non[zero]
                Llo, Lhi = lo_bound, hi_bound
                for __ in range(int(iters)):
                    L = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
                    ok = True
                    if unaff > L + tol:
                        ok = False
                    lo = 0.0
                    hi = 1.0
                    if ok and np.any(zero):
                        if float(bz.max()) > L + tol:
                            ok = False
                    if ok and np.any(pos):
                        v = float(np.min((L - bp) / ap))
                        if v < hi:
                            hi = v
                    if ok and np.any(neg):
                        v = float(np.max((L - bn) / an))
                        if v > lo:
                            lo = v
                    if lo < 0.0:
                        lo = 0.0
                    if hi > 1.0:
                        hi = 1.0
                    if ok and (lo > hi + tol or lo > 1.0 + tol or hi < -tol):
                        ok = False
                    if ok:
                        disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * (L - base_diag)
                        if disc >= 0.0:
                            rt = np.sqrt(disc)
                            r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - rt)
                            r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + rt)
                            if lo > r1 and hi < r2:
                                ok = False
                    if ok:
                        Lhi = L
                    else:
                        Llo = L
                L = Lhi
                lo = 0.0
                hi = 1.0
                if np.any(pos):
                    v = float(np.min((L - bp) / ap))
                    if v < hi:
                        hi = v
                if np.any(neg):
                    v = float(np.max((L - bn) / an))
                    if v > lo:
                        lo = v
                if lo < 0.0:
                    lo = 0.0
                if hi > 1.0:
                    hi = 1.0
                cands = []
                disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * (L - base_diag)
                if lo <= hi + tol:
                    if disc < 0.0:
                        cands.append(float(np.clip(x0, lo, hi)))
                    else:
                        rt = np.sqrt(disc)
                        r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - rt)
                        r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + rt)
                        if lo <= r1 + 1e-11:
                            xv = min(hi, r1)
                            if xv >= lo - 1e-11:
                                cands.append(float(np.clip(xv, 0.0, 1.0)))
                        if hi >= r2 - 1e-11:
                            xv = max(lo, r2)
                            if xv <= hi + 1e-11:
                                cands.append(float(np.clip(xv, 0.0, 1.0)))
                        if not cands:
                            cands.append(float(np.clip(x0, lo, hi)))
                x_best = x0
                d_best = 0.0
                for cv in cands:
                    dd = abs(cv - x0)
                    if dd < d_best:
                        d_best = dd
                        x_best = cv
                x_new = float(np.clip(x_best, 0.0, 1.0))
            if abs(x_new - x0) > 1e-12:
                new_aff = aff + (x_new - x0) * A
                new_aff[p] = base_diag + x_new - x_new * x_new
                c[m_min:m_max + 1] = new_aff
                h[p] = x_new
                omh[p] = 1.0 - x_new
                improved = True
        if improved:
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full").astype(float)
            omh = 1.0 - h
            cc = float(c.max())
            if cc < best_raw:
                best_raw = cc
                best = h.copy()
        else:
            break
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    cc = compute_c5(h, n)
    if cc * (n / 2.0) < best_raw:
        best = h.copy()
        best_raw = cc * (n / 2.0)
    return best, compute_c5(best, n)

def smooth_core(x, n, K, alpha, nu):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    m = float(c.max())
    if K < c.size:
        order = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:] if K > 0 else np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    else:
        order = np.arange(c.size)
    shift = alpha * (c[order] - m)
    ex = np.exp(shift)
    wsum = float(ex.sum())
    w = ex / wsum
    f_raw = m + np.log(wsum) / alpha
    scale = 2.0 / n
    sdiff = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    f = scale * f_raw + nu * sdiff * sdiff
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for idx, wt in zip(order, w):
        d = int(idx) - (n - 1)
        if d == 0:
            g += wt * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
        elif d > 0:
            l = n - d
            g[:l] += wt * (1.0 - x[d:])
            g[d:] -= wt * x[:l]
        else:
            p = -d
            l = n - p
            g[p:] += wt * (1.0 - x[:l])
            g[:l] -= wt * x[p:]
    return f, scale * g + 2.0 * nu * sdiff

def smooth_objective(x, n, K, alpha, nu):
    return smooth_core(x, n, K, alpha, nu)[0]

def smooth_gradient(x, n, K, alpha, nu):
    return smooth_core(x, n, K, alpha, nu)[1]

def lbfgs_polish(h, n, deadline, configs):
    best = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    for K, alpha, nu in configs:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_objective,
                best,
                args=(n, K, alpha, nu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                jac=smooth_gradient,
                options={"maxfun": 90, "maxiter": 45, "ftol": 1e-13}
            )
            hp = project_feasible(res.x, n)
            cp = compute_c5(hp, n)
            if cp < best_c:
                best = hp
                best_c = cp
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best, best_c

def pair_objective(h, n, p, q, x):
    s = h[p] + h[q]
    hh = h.copy()
    hh[p] = x
    hh[q] = s - x
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(hh, 1.0 - hh, mode="full")))

def pair_polish(h, n, deadline, pairs, seed):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best = h.copy()
    best_raw = float(np.max(np.correlate(best, 1.0 - best, mode="full")))
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    c = 0.6180339887498949
    for _ in range(int(pairs)):
        if time.time() > deadline or n < 2:
            break
        p = int(rng.randint(0, n - 1))
        q = p + 1
        s = h[p] + h[q]
        lo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
        hi = min(1.0, s)
        if hi - lo < 1e-12:
            continue
        x1 = hi - c * (hi - lo)
        x2 = lo + c * (hi - lo)
        y1 = pair_objective(h, n, p, q, x1)
        y2 = pair_objective(h, n, p, q, x2)
        for __ in range(30):
            if hi - lo < 1e-10:
                break
            if y1 < y2:
                hi = x2
                x2 = x1
                y2 = y1
                x1 = hi - c * (hi - lo)
                y1 = pair_objective(h, n, p, q, x1)
            else:
                lo = x1
                x1 = x2
                y1 = y2
                x2 = lo + c * (hi - lo)
                y2 = pair_objective(h, n, p, q, x2)
        x = float(np.clip(0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0))
        y = pair_objective(h, n, p, q, x)
        if y < best_raw - 1e-12:
            best = h.copy()
            best[p] = x
            best[q] = s - x
            best_raw = y
    return best, compute_c5(best, n)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        n_best = len(initial_h_values)
        best_h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        n_best = 160
        best_h = np.full(n_best, 0.5, dtype=float)
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n_best)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n_best)

    if time.time() < deadline - 450.0:
        dl = min(deadline - 420.0, time.time() + 220.0)
        h2, c2 = cd_fast(best_h, n_best, 80, dl, seed=seed, iters=16)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, n_best = h2, c2, len(h2)

    if time.time() < deadline - 220.0:
        dl = min(deadline - 210.0, time.time() + 60.0)
        pp = 24 if n_best <= 200 else 10 if n_best <= 500 else 6
        h2, c2 = pair_polish(best_h, n_best, dl, pp, seed=seed + 17)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, n_best = h2, c2, len(h2)

    expl_dl = deadline - 300.0
    for n_try in (24, 36, 48, 72, 112, 184, 304, 484):
        if time.time() > expl_dl - 30.0:
            break
        if n_try == n_best:
            continue
        max_passes = 3000 if n_try <= 36 else 1500 if n_try <= 72 else 700 if n_try <= 184 else 250
        n_starts = 10 if n_try <= 72 else 6 if n_try <= 184 else 3
        per = 12.0 if n_try <= 72 else 20.0 if n_try <= 184 else 35.0
        for s in range(n_starts):
            if time.time() > expl_dl - 20.0:
                break
            if s == 0:
                base = interp_to(best_h, n_best, n_try)
            else:
                base = random_start(n_try, s % 4, rng)
            base = project_feasible(base, n_try)
            dl = min(time.time() + per, expl_dl - 10.0)
            h2, c2 = cd_fast(base, n_try, max_passes, dl, seed=seed + 1000 * n_try + s)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, n_best = h2, c2, n_try
                if time.time() < deadline - 160.0:
                    up_n = min(2 * n_best, max(256, n_try * 2))
                    base_up = project_feasible(interp_to(best_h, n_best, up_n), up_n)
                    lu = min(deadline - 150.0, time.time() + 70.0)
                    h3, c3 = cd_fast(base_up, up_n, 250 if up_n <= 200 else 80, lu, seed=seed + 5000 + n_try)
                    if c3 < best_c:
                        best_h, best_c, n_best = h3, c3, up_n

    rest_dl = deadline - 90.0
    rr = 0
    while time.time() < rest_dl - 20.0 and rr < 60:
        s = 0.006 + 0.0025 * rr
        hp = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, s, n_best), n_best)
        if rr % 5 == 4:
            hp = project_feasible(binary_threshold(hp, n_best), n_best)
        maxp = 1200 if n_best <= 80 else 500 if n_best <= 200 else 120 if n_best <= 400 else 35
        per = 35.0 if n_best <= 120 else 25.0 if n_best <= 300 else 15.0
        dl = min(time.time() + per, rest_dl - 10.0)
        h2, c2 = cd_fast(hp, n_best, maxp, dl, seed=9000 + rr)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, n_best = h2, c2, len(h2)
            rr = max(0, rr - 3)
        rr += 1

    if time.time() < deadline - 45.0:
        dl = min(deadline - 35.0, time.time() + 40.0)
        pp = 30 if n_best <= 200 else 12 if n_best <= 500 else 6
        h2, c2 = pair_polish(best_h, n_best, dl, pp, seed=seed + 777)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, n_best = h2, c2, len(h2)

    if time.time() < deadline - 25.0:
        cfg = (
            (40, 800.0, 8.0 * n_best),
            (70, 4000.0, 25.0 * n_best),
            (110, 15000.0, 50.0 * n_best),
        )
        h2, c2 = lbfgs_polish(best_h, n_best, deadline - 18.0, cfg)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, n_best = h2, c2, len(h2)

    maxp = 3000 if n_best <= 80 else 1200 if n_best <= 180 else 400 if n_best <= 350 else 120
    h2, c2 = cd_fast(best_h, n_best, maxp, deadline - 1.0, seed=424242, iters=17)
    if c2 < best_c:
        best_h, best_c, n_best = h2, c2, len(h2)

    final_h = project_feasible(best_h, n_best)
    final_c = compute_c5(final_h, n_best)
    return final_h, final_c, n_best
```
